SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]

WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'

FROM [Tickets] AS [t]

2026-06-22 10:39:00.704 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[@p='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

2026-06-22 10:39:00.707 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC
2026-06-22 10:39:17.025 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (19ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC
2026-06-22 10:39:17.053 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

    SELECT TOP(1) [t].[Id], [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [t].[CreatedAt], [t].[Description], [t].[ProductId], [t].[Status], [t].[Title], [t].[UserId], [u].[Id] AS [Id0], [u].[Address], [u].[DateOfBirth], [u].[Email], [u].[FullName], [u].[ImageUrl], [u].[IsActive], [u].[MobileNumber], [u].[PasswordHash], [u].[Role], [u0].[Id] AS [Id1], [u0].[Address] AS [Address0], [u0].[DateOfBirth] AS [DateOfBirth0], [u0].[Email] AS [Email0], [u0].[FullName] AS [FullName0], [u0].[ImageUrl] AS [ImageUrl0], [u0].[IsActive] AS [IsActive0], [u0].[MobileNumber] AS [MobileNumber0], [u0].[PasswordHash] AS [PasswordHash0], [u0].[Role] AS [Role0], [p].[Id] AS [Id2], [p].[Description] AS [Description0], [p].[IsActive] AS [IsActive1], [p].[Name]
    FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
    INNER JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[UserId] = [u].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u0] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u0].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [Products] AS [p] ON [t].[ProductId] = [p].[Id]
    WHERE [t].[Id] = @ticketId
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [TicketsComments] AS [t0] ON [s].[Id] = [t0].[TicketId]
ORDER BY [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [s].[Id1], [s].[Id2]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC
2026-06-22 10:41:20.518 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]
2026-06-22 10:48:28.294 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (5ms) [Parameters=[@p='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC
2026-06-22 10:48:28.294 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [t0].[Key] AS [Date], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM (
    SELECT CONVERT(date, [t].[CreatedAt]) AS [Key]
    FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
) AS [t0]
GROUP BY [t0].[Key]
ORDER BY [t0].[Key]

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]
2026-06-22 11:08:49.164 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[@p='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

    FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
) AS [t0]
GROUP BY [t0].[Key]
